Warning foundation.claude.entrypoint (repo)
No CLAUDE.md found. Claude Code reads CLAUDE.md, not AGENTS.md directly — bridge it with a CLAUDE.md containing '@AGENTS.md'.
Why: Claude Code auto-loads only CLAUDE.md; without it Claude starts with no project memory.
Fix: Add a CLAUDE.md (or bridge an existing AGENTS.md with a CLAUDE.md containing '@AGENTS.md').
docs
Warning quality.examples.present (repo)
No fenced code example or preferred/avoided pair ('instead of', "don't ... do", 'prefer ... over') found in entry-point or instructions files.
Why: A single concrete example anchors ambiguous prose better than more prose.
Fix: Add one fenced code block showing a preferred pattern, or an 'instead of X, do Y' pair.
docs
Warning tooling.setup.script (repo)
No setup script found (scripts/setup*, bootstrap*, a 'setup' make target, or a devcontainer/Dockerfile).
Why: Agents need a deterministic way to bootstrap the environment before they can run anything.
Fix: Add a scripts/setup* or bootstrap* script, a 'setup' make target, or a devcontainer.
docs
Warning verify.build-command.documented (repo)
The repository has a build system, but no build command is documented in the entry point or instructions files.
Why: A repository with a build system but no documented build command forces agents to guess how to compile their changes.
Fix: Document the build command (e.g. `npm run build` or `cargo build`) in the entry point.
docs
Warning verify.test-command.documented (repo)
No test command is documented in the entry point or instructions files; agents cannot self-verify changes without one.
Why: An agent can only self-verify a change when the docs state the exact test command and that command actually exists.
Fix: Add the exact test command (e.g. `pytest` or `npm test`) to the entry point and make sure the test setup it names exists.
docs
Warning verify.test-suite.exists (repo)
No test suite evidence found (no pytest/JS test config, tests/ directory, test script/target, or tox.ini).
Why: Without a test suite there is no automated ground truth an agent can use to check its own work.
Fix: Add a test suite (e.g. a tests/ directory with a pytest or JS test config) and a test script.
docs
Warning agents.tools.declared .github/agents/agentic-workflows.md
Agent file declares no `tools` field, so it inherits every available tool.
Why: An agent without a `tools` declaration inherits every tool; least privilege limits blast radius and sharpens behavior.
Fix: Declare an explicit `tools:` list with only the tools the agent needs.
docs

Unknown agent frontmatter field 'disable-model-invocation'.
Why: Unknown frontmatter fields are silently ignored by the platforms, hiding typos of real fields.
Fix: Remove or rename frontmatter fields outside the known agent schema.

Only 0 of 42 directives explain why (because/since/to avoid/so that/otherwise/to ensure/to prevent); target is 25%.
Why: Directives that carry a rationale are followed more reliably and generalize better than bare imperatives.

Entry point documents 0 of 17 package.json scripts as inline code; document at least 2 (e.g. `npm run <script>`).

Warning verify.loop.instructed .github/copilot-instructions.md
The entry point never instructs the agent to run the tests and iterate until they pass.
Why: Agents follow explicit loops; without an instructed run-verify-iterate cycle they stop at the first plausible diff.
Fix: Add a sentence like 'Run the tests after every change and iterate until they pass' to the entry point.

Warning foundation.agentsmd.bridged AGENTS.md
AGENTS.md exists but no CLAUDE.md bridges it. Claude Code reads CLAUDE.md, not AGENTS.md, so this repository's AGENTS.md content is invisible to Claude Code.
Why: Claude Code reads CLAUDE.md, not AGENTS.md, so unbridged AGENTS.md content is invisible to it.
Fix: Create a CLAUDE.md containing '@AGENTS.md' to bridge the existing AGENTS.md.
